MA	<p>9550_6a01</p> <p>Restriction enzymes are proteins that cut DNA at specific sequences. <span style="color: #00775f;"><i>SplI</i></span> is derived from Spirulina platensis, and <span style="color: #0067cc;"><i>MunI</i></span> is derived from Mycoplasma species. Both enzymes are labeled on the map.</p><p><strong>DNA Strand Question:</strong></p><p>Examine the DNA strand shown below.
